Status: Done. Merged as PR #55; native Jira Done. Depends on merged QUILS-51 (PR #54).

After merged QUILS-51, complete the agreed v1 final notification workflow using the supported finite work/outbox system. Each distinct frozen email destination receives one message covering its sender, signer or recipient roles with the exact fully executed PDF and standalone certificate, plus its own party-bound readonly completion backup link. This is completion notification only; no account creation, signer/edit authority, lookup/resend endpoint, live sending, cloud resources or operator policy changes.

Create or resume once-only durable completion notification scope tied to the exact immutable publication/recipe/revision and frozen party identity. Queue only supported payload/template/dispatch handlers in this same slice. Parent-lock/current-source verification, canonical audit/idempotency and the existing transactional storage-hint protocol must prevent duplicate rows, lost notification work and unsupported mail. Preserve actual Completed/Finish/Date Signed, original sent PDF, own checkpoints and final stored versions. Ordinary readonly pages/file GETs never produce notifications or mutate domain state.

At dispatch, verify the current complete published pair and exact file versions/hashes/seals without obtaining private seal keys or rerendering/resealing. Use QUILS-51 copied bounded PDF attachments and QUILS-49 trusted caller-owned issuance for a fresh exact-party completion link, with protected scope and last-token references persisted and raw transport held only in memory and rendered as the QUILS-50 fragment/manual-code entry. Link/session lifetimes are bounded by actual current retention and independent of old signing expiry. Do not activate a link so early that queue delays consume its useful lifetime. Raw link tokens, protected payloads, private references and PDF bytes never enter public URLs/query strings, logs or audit presentation.

Follow requirements.md L6: if the verified pair exceeds provider/configured attachment limits, send an explicit link-only completion message and warn the sender in its own notification. Never silently drop attachments or claim they were attached. Missing/corrupt/stale source or invalid retention cannot trigger a link-only fallback. Verify and prepare all required source/party facts; each envelope/party has its own message and private link, without exposing another party's link or broadening capabilities.

Preserve disabled-email, suppression, verified destination, known rejection versus ambiguous submission, cancellation, original provider outcome and finite lease/retry semantics. Renew or replace expired unused transport only under a confirmed known-unsent state; uncertain provider acceptance is never blindly resent or replayed into a replacement session. Explicit recovery must not replace immutable PDFs or manufacture delivery/receipt proof. Guard lease/source scope before externally consequential submission and authoritative commits. Use event-driven finite work and storage-only recovery, never recurring idle SQL polling.

Test the genuine sequential two-signer production completion through durable once-only final messages for every frozen party using actual local Mailpit capture and decoded byte/hash equality. Exercise each private backup link through its scanner-safe explicit browser flow and readonly exact downloads after original signing expiry. Cover duplicate/recovery/restart, queue delays and link expiration, disabled configuration and suppression, byte/size fallback with sender warning, stale/corrupt/missing publication/source, rollback and uncertain commits/submission, lease loss/cancellation and no regeneration/private-key dependency. Validate real desktop/mobile/native-keyboard Mailpit-to-records flows using synthetic NO AGREEMENT data only. Run meaningful full/model/dependency/vendor/license/secret checks, commit/push/create/attach/pass exact-head checks/merge and native Jira Done.

Matching native Jira and docs/plan.md must exist and be updated before implementation or requirements changes. Start In Progress only after QUILS-51 merges on a separate QUILS-numbered branch/worktree. Keep dependencies permissive and PDFsharp/MigraDoc MIT 6.2.4. Existing infrastructure/email approvals remain with their owner; no Azure/AWS changes, live SES/TSA/email, purchases, fabricated operator disclosure/retention policies, optional OTP or v1 scope expansion.

Integration design: add a supported completion-ready email dispatch branch before producing its rows. Reuse WorkKind.Email and existing outbox fields with deterministic publication/primary-party IDs, protected static publication/recipe/revision/file-reference/party scope and an optional last-token reference; do not persist raw link codes or PDF bytes. One message covers each distinct normalized frozen email destination, choosing sender first, then the first signer, then a recipient as its stable narrow link party, so a sender who also signs receives one copy. Prepare rows, canonical queued evidence and storage hints in the final publication transaction when work is enabled; publish wakeups only after commit. Provide explicit finite resume for existing completed packets, preserving terminal and live-leased work without idle SQL discovery. Dispatch verifies the actual immutable pair before its parent-first work-lease/source/party checks, issues a fresh completion link and commits submission intent only for that attempt, then calls the existing email sender. Raw codes remain in memory for the confirmed attempt. Known rejected unused hashes expire naturally rather than modifying their immutable completion-token bindings; ambiguous submission is never replayed or reissued automatically. Render escaped HTML and plain text with two fixed safe PDF filenames and a fragment backup link; size-only fallback includes an explicit attachment warning in every link-only message, including the sender's. Use actual recorded completion/demo/retention facts, never fabricated policy. Register completion services lazily for worker-only/custom and unrelated-email hosts, keeping the default liveness and original session paths intact.

Regression validation clarification: fixed historical UTC-midnight fixtures have expired against the storage wall clock. Seed the upcoming UTC midnight before creating consent and derive expected dates from that same fixture clock, preserving all production session/chronology checks. Rerun review and Finish regression cases alongside completion notification validation.

Twelve new notification cases cover atomic publication/audit/hint rollback and lost commit, duplicate/concurrent resume, delayed issuance after signing expiry, distinct-destination grouping, decoded/encoded size warnings, disabled/suppressed delivery, corrupt/missing files, post-I/O work/party/evidence/lease changes, preparation cancellation, definite rejection, unknown acceptance and receipt-commit uncertainty without resend. Actual local Mailpit capture delivers the exact immutable pair to every destination; sender desktop and recipient mobile fragment access, signer manual entry and four native downloads verify exact bytes. Phone width has no horizontal overflow. Final inspection confirms two retained-file writes and two seals, with no regeneration. Model, pinned vendor/license, vulnerability and staged/full-history secret checks pass. Midnight fixtures now use the upcoming UTC boundary before consent; receipt fault injections are isolated per attempt. All 12 applicable exact-head CI checks passed before PR #55 merged. ## QUILS-55: Verify uploaded completed PDFs without granting record access

Status: Done. Merged as PR #58; native Jira Done. Depends on merged QUILS-45, QUILS-46 and QUILS-48; includes merged QUILS-54 (PR #57).

Implement the internal bounded readonly backend for the agreed v1 uploaded-PDF verification feature. It is independent of sender-void UI and uses the existing immutable final publication and seal verification foundations. The caller supplies exact uploaded PDF bytes/stream with a strict size bound; do not normalize, rewrite, persist or disclose the uploaded document. Match only its SHA-256 against a uniquely recognized retained Completed executed PDF or standalone certificate. Unknown, altered, nonfinal, expired-retention or ambiguous matches must never receive a known-packet success. Signing-session or signing-deadline expiry alone must not prevent verification of a still-retained completed packet.

Verify the recognized artifact against the canonical completed publication, exact immutable hash/file/seal bindings and public CMS/RFC3161 evidence, then recheck the current database scope after asynchronous work. Reuse the existing verification code where possible and fail closed on stale/corrupt/missing/unavailable records. No signer/sender/completion-reader session or private signing key acquisition is needed. Do not fetch URLs embedded in a PDF, perform live TSA/OCSP/CRL requests, call certificate providers for private keys, regenerate packets, queue email/work, mint capabilities or restore any user authority.

Return only minimal verification facts for the supplied artifact: whether it matches a retained published artifact, its kind and supplied SHA-256, and accurately bounded seal/timestamp integrity facts. Distinguish an unrecognized artifact from a verified integrity failure or temporary unavailability. Never disclose other envelopes, party identities, document downloads, raw audit/private metadata or storage references. Integrity checks do not assert signer identity, legal validity, QES/AdES status, external certificate trust or long-term revocation validation. No public upload endpoint, history lookup, UI or retention-policy choice is included in this backend slice; a later story owns the scanner-safe bounded HTTP/browser flow.

Before implementation, mirror this story in docs/plan.md on its own QUILS-numbered branch/worktree and move In Progress. Validate meaningful regressions, model, pinned vendors/licenses/vulnerabilities and staged/full-history secrets. Commit/push/create/attach/pass exact-head checks/merge and native Jira Done in the same pass. Keep dependencies permissive and PDFsharp/MigraDoc MIT 6.2.4. No cloud resources, live email/TSA, purchases, fabricated operator policies, optional OTP or scope expansion. Preserve separate infrastructure/email approval work.

## QUILS-56: Expose bounded readonly uploaded-PDF verification

Status: Done. Merged as PR #59; native Jira Done. Depends on merged QUILS-55 (PR #58).

After QUILS-55 merges, expose the agreed public /verify upload flow through its bounded readonly backend. A metadata-only GET shows what verification checks and does not access SQL, parse a document, prepare a result or enqueue work. The visitor explicitly selects one PDF and submits Verify; no account, signing agreement or new document/session/access capability is introduced. Keep result facts minimal and private to the current response, with no file contents, filename echo, party identity, other envelope metadata, private file references or document-download links.

Require antiforgery for multipart/browser and JSON POST clients, a dedicated bounded per-IP action limit plus a nonqueued global concurrency guard before multipart body buffering, and strict total request/file/form/header bounds. Keep upload processing in bounded memory for the accepted size, with no retained upload or temporary disk spill. Enforce exactly one PDF, the backend byte/header/declaration/deadline checks and cancellation. Use no-store/no-referrer/nosniff responses, no result/action/private data in URLs or logs, no GET mutation and no browser-autonomous resubmission. Do not change ordinary sender/signing/health or idle SQL behavior.

Show explicit neutral statuses for verified retained artifact, unrecognized bytes, rejected input, evidence that could not be verified and temporary unavailability. Explain that an unrecognized file may come from another instance, have expired retention or be changed, and does not by itself prove an invalid signature.
